Adult faces, limbs, garments, and furniture read as contour-enclosed matte color shapes with crisp overlaps and two-step values; contour and flat shadow shapes carry all form. Edo-period material patina appears only as bounded wear on physical costumes, props, and set surfaces. The lower prop strip holds the opened festival chest spilling paper banners and a dropped lacquer sake cup on compressed tatami, the central adult tableau shows their colliding bodies and the mask's wide-eyed painted face suspended mid-air, the upper architectural strip shows hanging washi lanterns and a dark night sky with distant fireworks over the river. Ai indigo anchor in his jacket and the night sky above, vermilion in her coat and the lantern glow, orpiment in scattered maple leaves and the firework traces, warm cream as skin base with bounded breast blush accent, malachite in the iris ornaments and a single lantern detail; one bokashi-style gradient confined to the upper sky strip, from deep ai indigo to warm cream with a single firework burst.
